    #search, .c-search_bg, .c-search_wrapper {
        transition: opacity 0.3s ease-in-out;
    }

    #search {
        pointer-events: none;
        position: fixed;
    }

    .c-search_bg, .c-search_wrapper {
        opacity: 0;
        pointer-events: none;

    }

    #search.is-active, #search.is-active > * {
        opacity: 1;
        display:flex;
        pointer-events: all;
    }
</style>

<script>
    const html = document.documentElement
    const search = document.querySelector('#search')
    const openSearchBtn = document.querySelector('#open-search')
    const closeSearchBtn = document.querySelector('#close-search')
    const searchBackground = document.querySelector('#search .c-search_bg')
    const searchInput = document.querySelector('#site-search')

    const closeSearch = () => {
        search.classList.remove('is-active')
        searchInput.blur()
        lenis.start()
        html.classList.remove('s')
    }

    if (search && openSearchBtn && closeSearchBtn && searchBackground && searchInput) {
        openSearchBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            search.classList.add('is-active')
            lenis.stop()
            html.classList.add('s')

            const focusNow = () => {
                searchInput.focus({preventScroll: true})
                searchInput.select()
            }

            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                requestAnimationFrame(focusNow)
            })
        })

        closeSearchBtn.addEventListener('click', closeSearch)
        searchBackground.addEventListener('click', closeSearch)
    }
</script>

<script>
    ;(() => {
        const form = document.querySelector('[data-search-form]')
        const input = document.querySelector('[data-search-input]')
        if (!form || !input) return
        form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
            const q = input.value.trim()
            if (!q) return
            window.location.href = `/search?query=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`
        })
    })()
</script>

<script>
    ;(() => {
        const input = document.querySelector('[data-search-input]')
        const list = document.querySelector('[fs-list-combine="search-panel"]')
        if (!input || !list) return

        const all = () => [...list.children]
        const hideAll = (arr) => arr.forEach(i => i.style.display = 'none')
        const show = (arr) => arr.forEach(i => i.style.display = '')

        const rand4 = () => {
            const arr = all()
            if (arr.length <= 4) return arr
            for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1))
                ;[arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]]
            }
            return arr.slice(0, 4)
        }

        const apply = () => {
            const items = all()
            hideAll(items)
            const q = input.value.trim().toLowerCase()
            if (!q) {
                show(rand4());
                return
            }
            let shown = 0
            for (const item of items) {
                if (shown >= 4) break
                const text = item.textContent.toLowerCase()
                if (text.includes(q)) {
                    item.style.display = '';
                    shown++
                }
            }
        }

        const obs = new MutationObserver(apply)
        obs.observe(list, {childList: true})

        apply()
        input.addEventListener('input', apply)
        setTimeout(apply, 150)
    })()
</script>

<script>
    ;(() => {
        const input = document.querySelector('[data-search-input]')
        const label = document.querySelector('[data-search-suggestion-label]')
        const list = document.querySelector('[data-search-suggestion]')
        const group = document.querySelector('.c-search_panel_group')
        const combine = document.querySelector('[fs-list-combine="search-panel"]')
        if (!input || !label || !list) return

        const baseText = label.textContent
        const notFoundText = 'Are looking for...'

        const norm = (s) => (s || '')
            .toLowerCase()
            .normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
            .replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ')
            .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
            .replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]+/g, '') // garde lettres/chiffres/espaces
            .trim()

        function subseqScore(q, s) {
            let qi = 0, si = 0, first = -1, last = -1
            while (qi < q.length && si < s.length) {
                if (q[qi] === s[si]) {
                    if (first === -1) first = si;
                    last = si;
                    qi++
                }
                si++
            }
            if (qi < q.length) return 0
            const span = last - first + 1
            return (q.length / span)
        }

        function matchScore(qRaw, sRaw) {
            const q = norm(qRaw), s = norm(sRaw)
            if (!q || !s) return 0
            if (s.startsWith(q)) return 3 + q.length / (q.length + 1)
            if (s.includes(q)) return 2 + q.length / (s.length + 1)
            const sub = subseqScore(q, s)
            return sub > 0 ? 1 + sub : 0
        }

        const items = () => [...list.children]
        const hideAll = () => items().forEach(el => el.style.display = 'none')
        const showSet = (set) => items().forEach(el => el.style.display = set.has(el) ? '' : 'none')

        function apply() {
            const hasCombine = combine && combine.children.length > 0
            const q = input.value
            const all = items()
            hideAll()

            if (!hasCombine) {
                if (group) {
                    group.style.display = 'none'
                }
                return
            }

            if (!q.trim()) {
                if (!all.length) {
                    label.style.display = 'none'
                    if (group) {
                        group.style.display = 'none'
                    }
                    return
                }
                if(group) {
                    group.style.display = 'flex'
                }
                label.textContent = baseText
                label.style.display = 'block'
                const arr = all.length <= 6 ? all : [...all].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5).slice(0, 6)
                arr.forEach(i => i.style.display = '')
                return
            }

            const scored = all.map(el => {
                const txtEl = el.querySelector('.label') || el
                const txt = txtEl ? txtEl.textContent : el.textContent
                return {el, score: matchScore(q, txt || '')}
            }).filter(x => x.score > 0)
                .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)
                .slice(0, 3)

            if (scored.length) {
                if (group) {
                    group.style.display = 'flex'
                }
                label.textContent = baseText
                label.style.display = 'none'
                showSet(new Set(scored.map(s => s.el)))
            } else {
                if (group) {
                    group.style.display = 'flex'
                }
                label.textContent = notFoundText
                label.style.display = 'block'
            }
        }

        label.style.display = 'block'
        hideAll()
        apply()
        input.addEventListener('input', apply)

        list.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const item = e.target.closest('[data-search-suggestion] > *')
            if (!item) return
            const txt = (item.querySelector('.label') || item).textContent.trim()
            if (!txt) return
            window.location.href = `/search?query=${encodeURIComponent(txt)}`
        })

        if (!!combine) {
            const obs = new MutationObserver(apply)
            obs.observe(combine, {childList: true})
        }
    })()
</script></div></div><section data-lenis-prevent="" class="s-age_gate"><div class="s-age_gate_form w-form"><form id="wf-form-Age-Gate" name="wf-form-Age-Gate" data-name="Age Gate" action="https://api-knr-davidoff.vercel.app/" method="post" class="s-age_gate_content" data-wf-page-id="689f1520b970d9d2058be490" data-wf-element-id="7f09c57f-6751-0494-7aa3-11b1f213a02b"><p class="text text-center">Please select your location of residence and year of birth to confirm you are of legal drinking age.</p><div class="c-fields"><div class="c-field"><select id="age-gate-country" name="country" data-name="country" required="" class="c-field__select w-select"><option value="">Select country</option><option value="France">France</option><option value="Switzerland">Switzerland</option></select></div><div class="c-field"><input class="c-field__input_number w-input" maxlength="256" name="age" data-name="age" placeholder="YYYY" type="text" id="age" required=""/></div></div><label class="w-checkbox"><input type="checkbox" name="remember" id="remember" data-name="remember" class="w-checkbox-input checkbox"/><span class="label w-form-label" for="remember">Remember me</span></label><p class="label-small text-center">By entering this site, you confirm that you are of legal drinking or purchasing age in your country or region of residence, or at least 21 years old where no such law exists. <script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const LOCALE = 'en';
  const PLACEHOLDER = 'Select country';
  const PRIORITY = ['CH','DE','FR','IT','ES','GB','US'];
  const EXCLUDE = ['AQ','BV','HM','TF','UM'];
  const select = document.getElementById('country') || document.querySelector('select#country, select[name="country"]');
  const jsonEl = document.getElementById('countries-json');
  if (!select || !jsonEl) return;
  let data;
  try { data = JSON.parse(jsonEl.textContent || '[]'); } catch { return; }
  let items = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
  items = items.map(x => {
    const value = String(x.iso2 || '').toUpperCase().trim();
    let label = String(x.name || '').trim();
    if (LOCALE && x.translations && x.translations[LOCALE]) label = String(x.translations[LOCALE]).trim() || label;
    return { value, label };
  }).filter(x => x.value && x.label && x.value.length === 2 && !EXCLUDE.includes(x.value));
  const seen = new Set();
  items = items.filter(x => seen.has(x.value) ? false : (seen.add(x.value), true));
  const head = PRIORITY.map(code => items.find(it => it.value === code)).filter(Boolean);
  const rest = items.filter(i => !PRIORITY.includes(i.value)).sort((a,b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label, LOCALE || 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' }));
  const sorted = head.concat(rest);
  select.innerHTML = '';
  const ph = new Option(PLACEHOLDER, '', true, true); ph.disabled = true; select.add(ph);
  for (const it of sorted) select.add(new Option(it.label, it.value));
});
</script>

<style>
.s-age_gate{display:none}
.no-scroll{overflow:hidden!important}
#age{text-align:center;letter-spacing:.21em;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;font-feature-settings:'tnum' 1}
#age::placeholder{letter-spacing:inherit;text-align:center}
</style>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const PATH_REGEX=/\/cognac/i;
  const COOKIE='dav_age_verified';
  const COOKIE_DAYS=30;

  const form=document.getElementById('wf-form-Age-Gate'); if(!form) return;
  const gate=form.closest('.s-age_gate')||form;
  const countrySel=form.querySelector('#age-gate-country');
  const yearInput=form.querySelector('#age');
  const done=(gate.querySelector('.w-form-done')||document.querySelector('.w-form-done'));
  const fail=(gate.querySelector('.w-form-fail')||document.querySelector('.w-form-fail'));
  const navs=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-wf--navbar--variant="transparent"]')];
  const navState=navs.map(el=>({el,was:el.classList.contains('scrolled')}));

  if(yearInput){
    const sanitize=v=>v.replace(/\D+/g,'').slice(0,4);
    yearInput.setAttribute('inputmode','numeric');
    yearInput.setAttribute('autocomplete','off');
    yearInput.setAttribute('maxlength','4');
    yearInput.setAttribute('pattern','\\d{4}');
    yearInput.addEventListener('input',e=>{
      const v=sanitize(e.target.value);
      if(e.target.value!==v) e.target.value=v;
    });
    yearInput.addEventListener('input', e => {
      const v = e.target.value.replace(/\D+/g,'').slice(0,4);
      if(e.target.value !== v) e.target.value = v;
    });
}

  const needsGate=PATH_REGEX.test(location.pathname);
  if(!needsGate || getCookie(COOKIE)==='1'){ hideGate(); return; }
  showGate();

  form.addEventListener('submit',(e)=>{
    e.preventDefault();
    const code=(countrySel?.value||'').toUpperCase();
    const ystr=(yearInput?.value||'');
    if(ystr.length!==4){ showFail(); return; }

    const year=parseInt(ystr,10), currentYear=new Date().getFullYear();
    let ages={}; try{ ages=JSON.parse((document.getElementById('age-map')?.textContent)||'{}'); }catch{}
    let minAge=ages[code]; if(minAge==null) minAge=21;
    const minBirthYear=currentYear-minAge;

    if(year<=minBirthYear){
      const remember=form.querySelector('input[name="remember"]')?.checked;
      setCookie(COOKIE,'1',remember?COOKIE_DAYS:1);
      hideGate(true);
      if(done) done.style.display='block';
    } else {
      showFail();
    }
  });

  function showGate(){
    gate.style.display='block';
    navs.forEach(n=>n.classList.add('scrolled'));
    if(window.lenis && typeof window.lenis.stop==='function'){ window.lenis.stop(); }
    else { document.documentElement.classList.add('no-scroll'); document.body.classList.add('no-scroll'); }
  }
  function hideGate(resume){
    gate.style.display='none';
    gate.classList.add('hidden');
    navState.forEach(({el,was})=>{ if(!was) el.classList.remove('scrolled'); });
    if(resume && window.lenis && typeof window.lenis.start==='function'){ window.lenis.start(); }
    else { document.documentElement.classList.remove('no-scroll'); document.body.classList.remove('no-scroll'); }
  }
  
  function setCookie(n,v,d){ const max=d*86400; document.cookie=`${n}=${v}; Path=/; Max-Age=${max}; SameSite=Lax`; }
  function getCookie(n){ const m=document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(?:^|; )'+n+'=([^;]*)')); return m?decodeURIComponent(m[1]):''; }
});
</script></div></section><div class="custom w-embed w-script"><script>
/* FILTER GROUPS */
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  /* RADIO-LIKE GROUPS */
  document.querySelectorAll('.c-filter_group[data-group-type="radio"] input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => {
    cb.addEventListener('change', e => {
      if (!e.target.checked) return
      const group = e.target.closest('.c-filter_group')
      group.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(other => {
        if (other !== e.target) other.checked = false
      })
    })
  })

  /* GROUP COUNT */
  document.querySelectorAll('.c-filter_group').forEach(g => {
    const badge = g.querySelector('[data-filter="group-count"]')
    if (!badge) return
    const update = () => {
      const n = g.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]:checked').length
      badge.textContent = n > 0 ? ` (${n})` : ''
    }
    g.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => {
      cb.addEventListener('change', update)
    })
    update()
  })
})
</script>

<script>
/* ACCORDION */
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-accordion]').forEach(g => {
    const header = g.querySelector('[data-accordion-header]')
    const content = g.querySelector('[data-accordion-content]')
    const svg = g.querySelector('[data-accordion-svg]')
    if (!header || !content) return

    if (g.getAttribute('data-accordion') === 'open') {
      content.style.setProperty('--height', content.scrollHeight+'px')
      if (svg) svg.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)'
    } else {
      g.setAttribute('data-accordion','close')
      content.style.setProperty('--height','0px')
      if (svg) svg.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)'
    }

    header.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const isOpen = g.getAttribute('data-accordion') === 'open'
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-accordion="open"]').forEach(open => {
        if (open !== g) {
          open.setAttribute('data-accordion','close')
          const c = open.querySelector('[data-accordion-content]')
          if (c) c.style.setProperty('--height','0px')
          const s = open.querySelector('[data-accordion-svg]')
          if (s) s.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)'
        }
      })
      if (isOpen) {
        g.setAttribute('data-accordion','close')
        content.style.setProperty('--height','0px')
        if (svg) svg.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)'
      } else {
        g.setAttribute('data-accordion','open')
        content.style.setProperty('--height', content.scrollHeight+'px')
        if (svg) svg.style.transform = 'rotate(180deg)'
      }
    })
  })
})
</script>

<script>
/* GROUP SYNC + URL PARAM */
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const src = document.querySelector('[data-group-sync="source"]')
  if (!src) return

  const updateURL = val => {
    const url = new URL(window.location.href)
    if (val === '') {
      url.searchParams.delete('category_equal')
    } else {
      url.searchParams.set('category_equal', val)
    }
    window.history.replaceState({}, '', url)
  }

  src.querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"][fs-list-value]').forEach(radio => {
    radio.addEventListener('change', () => {
      if (!radio.checked) return
      const val = radio.getAttribute('fs-list-value') || ''
      updateURL(val)
    })
  })

  // init: check if URL already has category_equal
  const current = new URL(window.location.href).searchParams.get('category_equal')
  if (current !== null) {
    const match = src.querySelector(`input[type="radio"][fs-list-value="${current}"]`)
    const empty = src.querySelector(`input[type="radio"][fs-list-value=""]`)
    if (match) match.checked = true
    else if (current === '' && empty) empty.checked = true
  }
})
</script>

<script>
/* FILTERS APPLIED COUNT */
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const badge=document.querySelector('[data-filter="filters-applied"]')
  const apply=document.querySelector('#apply-filter')
  const bg=document.querySelector('#filter .c-filter_bg')
  if(!badge||!apply) return

  const update=()=>{
    const groups=[...document.querySelectorAll('.c-filter_group')]
    const active=groups.filter(g=>g.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]:checked')).length
    badge.textContent=active>0?`(${active})`:''
  }

  apply.addEventListener('click',update)
  bg.addEventListener('click',update)
  update()
})
</script>

<script>
/* Active link in .s-plp_header except first */
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const currentSlug = window.location.pathname.replace(/^\/|\/$/g, "")
  const links = document.querySelectorAll(".s-plp_header a")
  links.forEach((link, i) => {
    if (i === 0) return
    const hrefSlug = link.getAttribute("href")?.replace(/^\/|\/$/g, "")
    if (hrefSlug === currentSlug) link.classList.add("is-list-active")
  })
})

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
    const PAGE_SLOT_SELECTOR = '[data-slot="product-insert"]'

    const isFirstPage = () => {
        const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search).toString()
        if (!q.includes("page")) {
            const pageCountEl = document.querySelector(".w-page-count")
            if (pageCountEl) {
                const text = pageCountEl.textContent.trim()
                const current = parseInt(text.split(/[\/]/)[0].trim(), 10)
                return current === 1
            }
            return true
        }
        // FIX 1: was q.includes("=1") — matches any param with =1 (ex: sort=1)
        return q.includes("page=1")
    }

    const isMobile = () => window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia(MOBILE_QUERY).matches

    const getColCount = g => {
        const cs = g && getComputedStyle(g).gridTemplateColumns
        if (!cs || cs === 'none') return 1
        return cs.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length
    }

    const variantToCols = (v, n) => {
        n = Math.max(1, n | 0)
        switch ((v || "").toLowerCase()) {
            case "l2": return n <= 2 ? { start: 1, span: Math.min(2, n) } : { start: 1, span: 2 }
            case "r2": return n <= 2 ? { start: 1, span: Math.min(2, n) } : { start: Math.max(1, n - 1), span: 2 }
            case "l3": return n <= 2 ? { start: 1, span: Math.min(2, n) } : { start: 1, span: Math.min(3, n) }
            case "r3": return n <= 2 ? { start: 1, span: Math.min(2, n) } : { start: Math.max(1, n - 2), span: Math.min(3, n) }
            default: return null
        }
    }

    const placeInsertsOnce = () => {
        const slot = document.querySelector(PAGE_SLOT_SELECTOR)
        const grid = document.querySelector(GRID_SELECTOR)
        if (!slot || !grid) return

        const toPlace = Array.from(slot.querySelectorAll(COMPONENT_SELECTOR)).filter(el => !el.dataset.inserted)
        if (!toPlace.length) return

        const used = new Set()
        const n = getColCount(grid)

        toPlace.sort((a, b) => (parseInt(a.getAttribute("data-row"), 10) || Infinity) - (parseInt(b.getAttribute("data-row"), 10) || Infinity))

        for (const el of toPlace) {
            const v = el.getAttribute("data-wf--product-insert--variant")
            const cols = variantToCols(v, n)
            if (!cols) continue
            let row = parseInt(el.getAttribute("data-row"), 10)
            if (!Number.isFinite(row) || row < 1) continue
            while (used.has(row)) row++
            used.add(row)
            el.style.gridColumn = `${cols.start} / span ${cols.span}`
            el.style.gridRow = String(row)
            if (!el.dataset.rowOriginal) el.dataset.rowOriginal = String(row)
            grid.appendChild(el)
            el.dataset.inserted = "1"
        }
    }

    const applyColsForAllInserts = grid => {
        if (!grid) return
        const n = getColCount(grid)
        grid.querySelectorAll(SELECT_INSERT).forEach(el => {
            const v = el.getAttribute("data-wf--product-insert--variant")
            const cols = variantToCols(v, n)
            if (!cols) return
            el.style.gridColumn = `${cols.start} / span ${cols.span}`
        })
    }

    const enforcePageVisibility = () => {
        const inserts = document.querySelectorAll(".c-product_insert")
        if (!inserts.length) return
        const show = isFirstPage()
        inserts.forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('hidden', !show))
    }

    const applyHideToggle = () => {
        const targets = document.querySelectorAll("[data-hide-product-insert]")
        const products = document.querySelectorAll(".c-product_insert")
        if (!targets.length || !products.length) return
        const anyActive = Array.from(targets).some(t => t.classList.contains("is-list-active"))
        products.forEach(el => el.classList.toggle("hidden", anyActive))
    }

    const getProductRows = grid => {
        const items = Array.from(grid.children).filter(el => getComputedStyle(el).display !== 'none' && !el.matches(SELECT_INSERT))
        const map = new Map()
        items.forEach(el => {
            const top = Math.round(el.getBoundingClientRect().top)
            if (!map.has(top)) map.set(top, [])
            map.get(top).push(el)
        })
        return Array.from(map.keys()).sort((a, b) => a - b)
    }

    const enforceMobileAspectRatio = () => {
        document.querySelectorAll(SELECT_INSERT).forEach(el => {
            const media = el.querySelector('img,video,picture,.c-product_insert_media')
            if (isMobile()) {
                el.style.aspectRatio = '361 / 492'
                if (media) media.style.aspectRatio = '361 / 492'
            } else {
                el.style.removeProperty('aspect-ratio')
                if (media) media.style.removeProperty('aspect-ratio')
            }
        })
    }

    const positionInsertsOnMobile = grid => {
        if (!grid || !isMobile()) return
        const n = getColCount(grid)
        const inserts = Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll(SELECT_INSERT))
            .sort((a, b) => (parseInt(a.dataset.rowOriginal, 10) || 0) - (parseInt(b.dataset.rowOriginal, 10) || 0))
        let prevRow = 1
        inserts.forEach(el => {
            el.style.display = ''
            el.style.gridColumn = `1 / span ${n}`
            const desired = parseInt(el.dataset.rowOriginal, 10) || 2
            const row = Math.max(2, desired, prevRow + 1)
            el.style.gridRow = String(row)
            prevRow = row
        })
    }

    const restoreDesktop = grid => {
        if (!grid || isMobile()) return
        grid.querySelectorAll(SELECT_INSERT).forEach(el => {
            el.style.display = ''
            if (el.dataset.rowOriginal) {
                el.style.gridRow = el.dataset.rowOriginal
            } else {
                el.style.removeProperty('grid-row')
            }
        })
    }

    const run = () => {
        enforcePageVisibility()
        const grid = document.querySelector(GRID_SELECTOR)
        if (!grid) return
        applyColsForAllInserts(grid)
        enforceMobileAspectRatio()
        // FIX 3: applyHideToggle was missing from run() — filter state wasn't reapplied on resize
        applyHideToggle()
        if (isMobile()) {
            positionInsertsOnMobile(grid)
        } else {
            restoreDesktop(grid)
        }
    }

    const handleRouteChange = () => {
        if (isFirstPage()) {
            placeInsertsOnce()
            applyHideToggle()
        }
        run()
    }

    handleRouteChange()

    const targets = document.querySelectorAll("[data-hide-product-insert]")
    if (targets.length) {
        const obs = new MutationObserver(applyHideToggle)
        targets.forEach(t => obs.observe(t, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class"] }))
    }

    const origPush = history.pushState, origReplace = history.replaceState
    history.pushState = function () { const r = origPush.apply(this, arguments); window.dispatchEvent(new Event('locationchange')); return r }
    history.replaceState = function () { const r = origReplace.apply(this, arguments); window.dispatchEvent(new Event('locationchange')); return r }
    window.addEventListener('popstate', () => window.dispatchEvent(new Event('locationchange')))
    window.addEventListener('locationchange', handleRouteChange)

    const grid = document.querySelector(GRID_SELECTOR)
    if (grid && 'ResizeObserver' in window) {
        const ro = new ResizeObserver(run)
        ro.observe(grid)
    } else {
        let t
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(run, 80) })
    }
    const mql = window.matchMedia(MOBILE_QUERY)
    if (mql && mql.addEventListener) mql.addEventListener('change', run)
    else if (mql && mql.addListener) mql.addListener(run)

    // FIX 4: stray console.debug was here in the original (outside IIFE), removed

    ;(() => {
        const targetSelector = '[data-filter=filters-applied]'

        const toggleProducts = () => {
            const f = document.querySelector(targetSelector)
            if (!f) return
            const hasNumber = /\(\s*\d+\s*\)/.test(f.textContent)
            document.querySelectorAll('.c-product_insert').forEach(el => {
                el.classList.toggle('hidden', hasNumber)
            })
        }

        const f = document.querySelector(targetSelector)
        if (!f) return

        const observer = new MutationObserver(toggleProducts)
        observer.observe(f, { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true })

        toggleProducts()
    })()

    // FIX 5: all stray console.debug / console.log removed throughout
    ;(() => {
        const pageCountEl = document.querySelector('.w-page-count')
        if (!pageCountEl) return

        const observer = new MutationObserver(mutations => {
            for (const mutation of mutations) {
                if (mutation.type === 'childList' || mutation.type === 'characterData') {
                    handleRouteChange()
                    break
                }
            }
        })

        observer.observe(pageCountEl, { childList: true, characterData: true, subtree: true })
    })()
})
</script></div><div class="hidden w-embed w-script"><script>
(function () {
  function hideSectionsIfEmpty(root = document) {
    var targets = root.querySelectorAll('[data-hide-section-when-empty]');
    targets.forEach(function (el) {
      var isEmpty = !!el.querySelector('.w-dyn-empty');
      var section = el.closest('section');
      if (!section) return;
      section.style.display = isEmpty ? 'none' : '';
    });
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    hideSectionsIfEmpty();
    var obs = new MutationObserver(function (mutations) {
      var shouldRecheck = mutations.some(function (m) {
        return Array.from(m.addedNodes || []).some(function (n) {
          return n.nodeType === 1 && (n.matches('.w-dyn-empty, .w-dyn-items') || n.querySelector?.('.w-dyn-empty, .w-dyn-items'));
        }) || Array.from(m.removedNodes || []).some(function (n) {
          return n.nodeType === 1 && (n.matches?.('.w-dyn-empty, .w-dyn-items') || n.querySelector?.('.w-dyn-empty, .w-dyn-items'));
        });
      });
      if (shouldRecheck) hideSectionsIfEmpty(document);
    });
    obs.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  });
})();
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const mq = matchMedia('(max-width: 991px)')
  const container = document.querySelector('.c-product_thumbnail')
  if (!container) return
  const baseImg = container.querySelector('img.thumbnail') || container.querySelector('img')
  const gallery = document.querySelector('.c-product_gallery')
  const svgProduct = document.querySelector('.c-svg_product')
  if (!gallery || !baseImg) return
  const thumbs = Array.from(gallery.querySelectorAll('[data-el="product-gallery-image"]'))
  if (!thumbs.length) return

  let stack = null
  let layers = []

  const buildStack = () => {
    if (stack) return
    stack = document.createElement('div')
    stack.className = 'c-card_stack'
    container.appendChild(stack)
    layers = thumbs.map((t, i) => {
      const img = document.createElement('img')
      ;['src','srcset','sizes','alt'].forEach(a => {
        const v = t.getAttribute(a)
        if (v) img.setAttribute(a, v)
      })
      img.decoding = 'async'
      img.loading = i === 0 ? 'eager' : 'lazy'
      img.style.objectFit = i === 0 ? 'contain' : 'cover'
      img.style.mixBlendMode = 'cover'
      stack.appendChild(img)
      return img
    })
  }

  const fadeInStack = () => {
    if (!stack) return
    void stack.offsetWidth
    container.classList.add('has-stack-active')
  }

  const fadeOutAndRemoveStack = () => {
    if (!stack) return
    const el = stack
    const onEnd = () => {
      el.removeEventListener('transitionend', onEnd)
      if (!container.classList.contains('has-stack-active')) {
        el.remove()
        stack = null
        layers = []
      }
    }
    el.addEventListener('transitionend', onEnd)
    container.classList.remove('has-stack-active')
  }

  const setActive = idx => {
    if (idx === 0) {
      thumbs.forEach((t, i) => t.classList.toggle('is-active', i === 0))
      svgProduct.style.opacity = "1"
      fadeOutAndRemoveStack()
      return
    }
    svgProduct.style.opacity = "0"
    if (!stack) {
      buildStack()
      fadeInStack()
    } else if (!container.classList.contains('has-stack-active')) {
      fadeInStack()
    }
    layers.forEach((l, i) => {
      l.classList.toggle('is-active', i === idx)
      l.style.objectFit = i === 0 ? 'contain' : 'cover'
    })
    thumbs.forEach((t, i) => t.classList.toggle('is-active', i === idx))
  }

  const onThumbClick = e => {
    if (!mq.matches) return
    const el = e.target.closest('[data-el="product-gallery-image"]')
    if (!el) return
    const idx = thumbs.indexOf(el)
    if (idx < 0) return
    if (idx === 0) { setActive(0); return }
    if (!stack) buildStack()
    const layer = layers[idx]
    if (!layer) return
    if (layer.complete) setActive(idx)
    else layer.addEventListener('load', () => setActive(idx), { once: true })
  }

  const init = () => { setActive(0) }

  gallery.addEventListener('click', onThumbClick, { passive: true })
  mq.addEventListener ? mq.addEventListener('change', init) : mq.addListener(init)
  init()
})
</script></div><div class="hidden w-embed w-script"><script>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  function findVideo(btn) {
    const sel = btn.dataset.target;
    if (sel) {
      const target = document.querySelector(sel);
      if (!target) return null;
      if (target.tagName && target.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'video') return target;
      const vSel = target.querySelector('video[data-active]') || target.querySelector('video');
      return vSel || null;
    }
    const wrap = btn.closest('section, .section') || document;
    return wrap.querySelector('video[data-active]') || wrap.querySelector('video');
  }

  function updateIcons(btn, isUnmuted) {
    const open  = btn.querySelector('.c-svg.open');
    const close = btn.querySelector('.c-svg.close');
    if (open)  open.classList.toggle('is-hidden', !isUnmuted);
    if (close) close.classList.toggle('is-hidden', isUnmuted);
    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(isUnmuted));
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('.c-button-sound').forEach(function (btn) {
    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    const v = findVideo(btn);
    if (v) {
      v.muted = true;
      updateIcons(btn, !v.muted);
    }

    btn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      e.preventDefault();
      const video = findVideo(btn);
      if (!video) return;
      const willUnmute = video.muted;
      video.muted = !willUnmute;
      if (willUnmute) {
        video.play?.().catch(function(){});
      }
      updateIcons(btn, willUnmute);
    }, false);
  });
});
</script></div><div class="hidden w-embed w-script"><style>
@media (min-width: 1024px) {
  .s-recipes__list > *:nth-child(even) {
    flex-direction: row-reverse;

      }      
        [data-panel] {pointer-events : none;}
  [data-panel] [data-el="panel-bg"],[data-panel]  [data-el="panel-inner"]   {
      	opacity:0;
        pointer-events : none;
      }
</style>
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const $ = (s, c=document) => c.querySelector(s);
  const $$ = (s, c=document) => Array.from(c.querySelectorAll(s));
  const getPanel = (slug) => $(`[data-panel="${slug}"]`);
  const getBg = (p) => p && $(`[data-el="panel-bg"]`, p);
  const getSubs = (p) => p ? $$(`[data-el="panel-inner"]`, p) : [];
  const getSub = (p, slug) => p && $(`[data-el="panel-inner"][data-recipe-slug="${slug}"]`, p);
	const html = document.documentElement

  const show = (el) => {
    if (!el) return;
    el.style.opacity = "1";
    el.style.pointerEvents = "auto";
  };
  const hide = (el) => {
    if (!el) return;
    el.style.opacity = "0";
    el.style.pointerEvents = "none";
  };
  const closeSubs = (p) => getSubs(p).forEach(hide);

  const stopScroll = () => {lenis.stop();html.classList.add('s')};
  const startScroll = () => {lenis.start();html.classList.remove('s')};

  const openPanel = (slug, recipe) => {
    const p = getPanel(slug);
    if (!p) return;
    show(getBg(p));
    closeSubs(p);
    if (recipe) show(getSub(p, recipe));
    stopScroll();
  };

  const closePanel = (p) => {
    if (!p) return;
    hide(getBg(p));
    closeSubs(p);
    startScroll();
  };

  $$('[data-action]').forEach(b => {
    b.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const a = b.dataset.action;

      if (a === 'open-panel') {
        const ps = b.dataset.panelType;
        const rs = b.dataset.recipeSlug || ps;
        openPanel(ps, rs);
      }

      if (a === 'close-panel') {
        let p = b.closest('[data-panel]') || getPanel(b.dataset.panelType);
        closePanel(p);
      }

      if (a === 'open-sub-panel') {
        const rs = b.dataset.recipeSlug;
        const p = b.closest('[data-panel]');
        if (!p || !rs) return;
        closeSubs(p);
        show(getSub(p, rs));
      }
    });
  });
});

<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        class Superzoom {
            static index = (el, arr) => {
                const n = arr.length
                for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (el === arr[i]) return i
                return 0
            }

            static direction = {
                left: 'left',
                right: 'right',
            }

            static cursor = {
                in: 'in',
                out: 'out',
            }

            static $ = (selectors) => {
                return document.querySelector(selectors)
            }

            static $$ = (selectors) => {
                return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(selectors))
            }

            static bind = (t, f) => {
                f.forEach((T) => {
                    t[T] = t[T].bind(t)
                })
            }

            constructor() {
                Superzoom.bind(this, [
                    'handleMouseEnter','handleMouseLeave','handleImageClick','handleImageMobileClick',
                    'handleSuperZoomClick','closeSuperzoom','handleClickArrowLeft','handleClickArrowRight',
                    'handleThumbnailHover','setMobileIndex',
                    'onTouchStart','onTouchMove','onTouchEnd','applyTransform','resetTransform'
                ])

                this.mainImage = Superzoom.$('.c-product_thumbnail img')
                this.mainImageWrapper = Superzoom.$('.c-product_thumbnail')


                this.images = [this.mainImage, ...Superzoom.$$('.c-product_gallery .product_image img')]

                if (Superzoom.$$('.c-product_gallery .product_image img').length === 0) return
                
                this.states = {
                    hasBuilt: false,
                    hasInit: false,
                }
                this.gesture = {
                    scale: 1,
                    min: 1,
                    max: 3,
                    x: 0,
                    y: 0,
                    startDist: 0,
                    startScale: 1,
                    midX: 0,
                    midY: 0,
                    isPinching: false,
                    isPanning: false,
                    lastX: 0,
                    lastY: 0
                }

                this.build()

                this.mouseArea = document.querySelector('.s-product__left')

                this.el = {
                    superzoom: Superzoom.$('[data-module-superzoom]'),
                    closeBtn: Superzoom.$('[data-superzoom="close_btn"]'),
                    imagesWrapper: Superzoom.$('[data-superzoom="images"]'),
                    images: Superzoom.$$('[data-superzoom="image"]'),
                    thumbnails: Superzoom.$$('[data-superzoom="thumbnail"]'),
                    arrowLeft: Superzoom.$('[data-superzoom="arrow_left"]'),
                    arrowRight: Superzoom.$('[data-superzoom="arrow_right"]'),
                }


                this.clickNumber = 0
                this.currenIndex = 0
                this.mobileIndex = 0


                this.updateMainImage()
                this.e()
            }

            get currentMainImage() {
                return this.el.images[this.currenIndex]
            }

            get isMobile() {
                return innerWidth < 1024
            }

            build() {
                if (this.states.hasBuilt) return
                const root = Superzoom.$('.global')
                const cr = (el, attribute, value = true) => {
                    const e = document.createElement(el)
                    if (attribute) {
                        e.setAttribute(attribute, value)
                    }
                    return e
                }

                const closeSvg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"><path d="M6 6L18 18" stroke="black" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path><path d="M18 6L6 18" stroke="black" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"></path></svg>`
                const arrowLeftSvg = `<svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M14.6082 6.7811C14.8315 6.9435 14.8809 7.256 14.7186 7.4793L10.9431 12.6697L14.7049 17.3729C14.8774 17.5885 14.8424 17.9035 14.6268 18.076C14.4111 18.2485 14.0961 18.2135 13.9236 17.9979L9.68534 12.7L13.9099 6.8914C14.0724 6.6681 14.3849 6.6187 14.6082 6.7811Z" fill="black"/></svg>`
                const arrowRightSvg = `<svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M9.39179 17.2189C9.16849 17.0565 9.11907 16.744 9.28144 16.5207L13.0569 11.3303L9.29511 6.62715C9.12261 6.41152 9.15761 6.09653 9.37324 5.92403C9.58887 5.75152 9.90386 5.78652 10.0764 6.00215L14.3147 11.3L10.0901 17.1086C9.92763 17.3319 9.61512 17.3813 9.39179 17.2189Z" fill="black"/></svg>`
                const superzoom = cr('div', 'data-module-superzoom')
                const navigation = cr('div', 'data-superzoom', 'navigation')
                const arrowLeft = cr('div', 'data-superzoom', 'arrow_left')
                const arroRight = cr('div', 'data-superzoom', 'arrow_right')
                const closeBtn = cr('div', 'data-superzoom', 'close_btn')
                const images = cr('div', 'data-superzoom', 'images')
                const thumbnails = cr('div', 'data-superzoom', 'thumbnails')

                this.images.forEach(image => {
                    const imageSrc = image.src

                    const mainImageWrapper = cr('div', 'data-superzoom', 'image')
                    const thumbnailImageWrapper = cr('div', 'data-superzoom', 'thumbnail')

                    const mainImage = cr('img')
                    const thumbnailImage = cr('img')

                    mainImage.src = imageSrc
                    thumbnailImage.src = imageSrc

                    mainImageWrapper.appendChild(mainImage)
                    thumbnailImageWrapper.appendChild(thumbnailImage)

                    images.appendChild(mainImageWrapper)
                    thumbnails.appendChild(thumbnailImageWrapper)
                })

                arrowLeft.innerHTML = arrowLeftSvg
                arroRight.innerHTML = arrowRightSvg

                navigation.appendChild(arrowLeft)
                navigation.appendChild(arroRight)

                closeBtn.innerHTML = closeSvg


                superzoom.appendChild(images)
                superzoom.appendChild(thumbnails)
                superzoom.appendChild(navigation)
                superzoom.appendChild(closeBtn)

                root.appendChild(superzoom)

                this.states.hasBuilt = true
            }

            updateMainImage(index = 0) {
                const isSameIndex = this.currenIndex === index
                if (isSameIndex && this.states.hasInit) return
                this.resetZoom()
                this.el.images.forEach((image) => {
                    image.style.display = 'none'
                })
                const currentImage = this.el.images[index]
                if (currentImage) currentImage.style.display = ''

                this.el.thumbnails.forEach(thumbnail => {
                    thumbnail.classList.remove('is-active')
                })

                const currentThumbnail = this.el.thumbnails[index]
                currentThumbnail.classList.add('is-active')

                this.currenIndex = index
                this.states.hasInit = true
            }

            handleMouseEnter() {
            }

            handleMouseLeave() {
            }

            openSuperzoom() {
                this.el.superzoom.classList.add('is-active')
                const html = document.documentElement
                lenis.stop()
                html.classList.add('s')
            }

            closeSuperzoom(e) {
                e.stopPropagation()
                this.resetZoom()
                this.el.superzoom.classList.remove('is-active')
                const html = document.documentElement
                lenis.start()
                html.classList.remove('s')
            }

            handleClickArrow(direction) {
                const isRight = direction === Superzoom.direction.right
                const total = this.images.length
                let index
                if (isRight) {
                    index = (this.currenIndex + 1) % total
                } else {
                    index = (this.currenIndex - 1 + total) % total
                }

                this.updateMainImage(index)
            }

            handleClickArrowLeft(e) {
                e.stopPropagation()

                this.handleClickArrow(Superzoom.direction.left)
            }

            handleClickArrowRight(e) {
                e.stopPropagation()

                this.handleClickArrow(Superzoom.direction.right)
            }

            handleThumbnailHover(e) {
                const tg = e.currentTarget
                const index = Superzoom.index(tg, this.el.thumbnails)
                const isSameIndex = this.currenIndex === index
                if (isSameIndex) return

                this.updateMainImage(index)
            }

            handleImageClick(e) {
                const tg = e.currentTarget
                const index = Superzoom.index(tg, this.images)
                this.openSuperzoom()
                this.updateMainImage(index)
            }

            handleImageMobileClick(e) {
                this.openSuperzoom()
                this.updateMainImage(this.mobileIndex)
            }

            updateCursor(kind) {
                const isZoomOut = kind === Superzoom.cursor.out
                if (isZoomOut) {
                    this.el.superzoom.classList.add('zoom-out')
                } else {
                    this.el.superzoom.classList.remove('zoom-out')
                }
            }

            resetZoom() {
                this.currentMainImage.classList.remove('zoom-1')
                this.currentMainImage.classList.remove('zoom-2')
                this.clickNumber = 0

                this.resetTransform()

            }

            handleSuperZoomClick() {
                this.clickNumber++

                if (this.clickNumber === 1) {
                    this.currentMainImage.classList.add('zoom-1')
                    this.updateCursor(Superzoom.cursor.in)
                } else if (this.clickNumber === 2) {
                    this.currentMainImage.classList.add('zoom-2')
                    this.updateCursor(Superzoom.cursor.out)
                } else if (this.clickNumber === 3) {
                    this.resetZoom()
                    this.updateCursor(Superzoom.cursor.in)
                }
            }

            setMobileIndex(e) {
                const tg = e.currentTarget
                this.mobileIndex = Superzoom.index(tg, this.images)
            }

            e() {
                this.mouseArea.addEventListener('mouseenter', this.handleMouseEnter)
                this.mouseArea.addEventListener('mouseleave', this.handleMouseLeave)


                this.el.closeBtn.addEventListener('click', this.closeSuperzoom)
                this.el.arrowLeft.addEventListener('click', this.handleClickArrowLeft)
                this.el.arrowRight.addEventListener('click', this.handleClickArrowRight)

                if (!this.isMobile) {
                    this.el.superzoom.addEventListener('click', this.handleSuperZoomClick)
                }

                this.el.thumbnails.forEach((thumbnail) => {
                    thumbnail.addEventListener('mouseenter', this.handleThumbnailHover)
                })

                if (this.isMobile) {
                    this.images.forEach((image) => {
                        image.addEventListener('click', this.setMobileIndex)
                    })
                    this.mainImageWrapper.addEventListener('click', this.handleImageMobileClick)
                } else {
                    this.images.forEach((image) => {
                        image.addEventListener('click', this.handleImageClick)
                    })
                }

                if(this.isMobile){
                    // prevent scroll while manipulating
                    this.el.superzoom.addEventListener('touchstart', this.onTouchStart, {passive:false})
                    this.el.superzoom.addEventListener('touchmove', this.onTouchMove, {passive:false})
                    this.el.superzoom.addEventListener('touchend', this.onTouchEnd, {passive:false})
                    this.el.superzoom.addEventListener('touchcancel', this.onTouchEnd, {passive:false})
                }


            }

            // PINCH
            getDistance(touches){
                const dx = touches[0].clientX - touches[1].clientX
                const dy = touches[0].clientY - touches[1].clientY
                return Math.hypot(dx, dy)
            }

            getMidpoint(touches){
                return {
                    x: (touches[0].clientX + touches[1].clientX) / 2,
                    y: (touches[0].clientY + touches[1].clientY) / 2
                }
            }

            applyTransform(){
                const el = this.currentMainImage
                el.style.transform = `scale(${this.gesture.scale})`
                this.updateCursor(this.gesture.scale > 1 ? Superzoom.cursor.out : Superzoom.cursor.in)
            }

            resetTransform(){
                const el = this.currentMainImage
                el.style.transform = ''
                this.gesture.scale = 1
                this.gesture.x = 0
                this.gesture.y = 0
                this.gesture.isPinching = false
                this.gesture.isPanning = false
            }

            onTouchStart(e){
                if(!this.isMobile) return
                if(!this.el.superzoom.classList.contains('is-active')) return
                if(e.touches.length === 2){
                    const d = this.getDistance(e.touches)
                    const mid = this.getMidpoint(e.touches)
                    this.gesture.isPinching = true
                    this.gesture.startDist = d
                    this.gesture.startScale = this.gesture.scale
                    this.gesture.midX = mid.x
                    this.gesture.midY = mid.y
                    e.preventDefault()
                } else if(e.touches.length === 1 && this.gesture.scale > 1){
                    this.gesture.isPanning = true
                    this.gesture.lastX = e.touches[0].clientX
                    this.gesture.lastY = e.touches[0].clientY
                    e.preventDefault()
                }
            }

            onTouchMove(e){
                if(!this.isMobile) return
                if(!this.el.superzoom.classList.contains('is-active')) return

                if(this.gesture.isPinching && e.touches.length === 2){
                    const d = this.getDistance(e.touches)
                    const mid = this.getMidpoint(e.touches)
                    let nextScale = this.gesture.startScale * (d / this.gesture.startDist)
                    if(nextScale < this.gesture.min) nextScale = this.gesture.min
                    if(nextScale > this.gesture.max) nextScale = this.gesture.max

                    // keep translation relative to midpoint drift
                    this.gesture.x += (mid.x - this.gesture.midX)
                    this.gesture.y += (mid.y - this.gesture.midY)
                    this.gesture.midX = mid.x
                    this.gesture.midY = mid.y

                    this.gesture.scale = nextScale
                    this.applyTransform()
                    e.preventDefault()
                } else if(this.gesture.isPanning && e.touches.length === 1 && this.gesture.scale > 1){
                    const cx = e.touches[0].clientX
                    const cy = e.touches[0].clientY
                    this.gesture.x += (cx - this.gesture.lastX)
                    this.gesture.y += (cy - this.gesture.lastY)
                    this.gesture.lastX = cx
                    this.gesture.lastY = cy
                    this.applyTransform()
                    e.preventDefault()
                }
            }

            onTouchEnd(e){
                if(!this.isMobile) return

                if(e.touches.length === 0){
                    if(this.gesture.scale <= 1.01){
                        this.resetTransform()
                        this.resetZoom()
                        this.updateCursor(Superzoom.cursor.in)
                    }
                    this.gesture.isPinching = false
                    this.gesture.isPanning = false
                } else if(e.touches.length === 1){
                    this.gesture.isPinching = false
                    if(this.gesture.scale > 1){
                        this.gesture.isPanning = true
                        this.gesture.lastX = e.touches[0].clientX
                        this.gesture.lastY = e.touches[0].clientY
                    }
                }
            }


        }

        const superzoom = new Superzoom()

    })
</script></div><div class="hidden w-embed w-script"><script>
    class Video {
        constructor() {
            this.videos = [...document.querySelectorAll("video")].filter(v =>
                v.querySelector("source[data-device]")
            );

            if (this.videos.length === 0) return;

            this.observer = new IntersectionObserver(
                entries => {
                    entries.forEach(entry => {
                        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                            this.updateVideo(entry.target);
                            entry.target.play?.().catch(() => {});
                            this.observer.unobserve(entry.target);
                        }
                    });
                });

            this.videos.forEach(v => this.observer.observe(v));

            window.addEventListener("resize", () => this.onResize());
        }

        getDevice() {
            return window.innerWidth <= 767 ? "m" : "d";
        }

        updateAll() {
            const device = this.getDevice();
            this.videos.forEach(video => this.updateVideo(video, device));
        }

        updateVideo(video, device = this.getDevice()) {
            const sources = video.querySelectorAll("source[data-device]");

            sources.forEach(source => {
                const dev = source.dataset.device;
                const src = source.dataset.src;

                if (dev === device || dev === "all") {
                    if (source.src !== src) {
                        source.src = src;
                        video.load();
                    }
                } else {
                    source.removeAttribute("src");
                }
            });
        }

        onResize() {
            clearTimeout(this.resizeTimeout);
            this.resizeTimeout = setTimeout(() => this.updateAll(), 200);
        }
    }

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => new Video());
</script></div><div class="hidden w-embed w-script"><script>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const groups = new Map();

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-module="accordion"]').forEach((accordion) => {
    const group = accordion.closest('section') || document.body;
    if (!groups.has(group)) groups.set(group, []);
    groups.get(group).push(accordion);
  });

  groups.forEach((accordions) => {
    accordions.forEach((accordion, index) => {
      const trigger = accordion.querySelector('[data-accordion="trigger"]');
      const content = accordion.querySelector('[data-accordion="content"]');
      const inner = content.querySelector('.c-accordion__content_inner');
      const indicator = trigger.querySelector('.c-accordion__indicator > *');

      const setHeight = () => {
        content.style.setProperty('--height', inner.scrollHeight + 'px');
      };

      const swapIndicator = (el, symbol) => {
        el.classList.add('is-fading');
        setTimeout(() => {
          el.textContent = symbol;
          el.classList.remove('is-fading');
        }, 150);
      };

      setHeight();

      if (index === 0) {
        content.classList.add('is-active');
        trigger.classList.add('is-active');
        indicator.textContent = '−';
      }

      trigger.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isActive = content.classList.contains('is-active');

        accordions.forEach((other) => {
          const otherContent = other.querySelector('[data-accordion="content"]');
          const otherTrigger = other.querySelector('[data-accordion="trigger"]');
          const otherIndicator = otherTrigger.querySelector('.c-accordion__indicator > *');

          if (otherContent.classList.contains('is-active')) {
            otherContent.classList.remove('is-active');
            otherTrigger.classList.remove('is-active');
            swapIndicator(otherIndicator, '+');
          }
        });

        if (!isActive) {
          setHeight();
          content.classList.add('is-active');
          trigger.classList.add('is-active');
          swapIndicator(indicator, '−');
        }
      });
    });

    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
      accordions.forEach((accordion) => {
        const content = accordion.querySelector('[data-accordion="content"]');
        const inner = content.querySelector('.c-accordion__content_inner');
        content.style.setProperty('--height', inner.scrollHeight + 'px');
      });
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
  const DATA_EL_ID = 'retailers-fragrances';

  const root = document.querySelector('.c-where_to_buy__main');
  if (!root) return;

  const shopGroup = root.querySelector('.c-shop_group');
  const storeGroup = root.querySelector('.c-store_group');
  const shopContent = shopGroup?.querySelector('.c-group_content');
  const storeContent = storeGroup?.querySelector('.c-group_content');
  const separator = root.querySelector('.c-separator');
  const unavailable = root.querySelector('.c-store_unavailable');

  let DATA = {};
  try { DATA = JSON.parse(document.getElementById(DATA_EL_ID)?.textContent || '{}'); } catch {}
  const ONLINE = DATA.ONLINE || {};
  const OFFLINE = DATA.OFFLINE || {};

  const has = cc =>
    (Array.isArray(ONLINE[cc]) && ONLINE[cc].length) ||
    (Array.isArray(OFFLINE[cc]) && OFFLINE[cc].length);

  const country = await detectCountry();
  const onlineItems = has(country) ? (ONLINE[country] || []) : [];
  const offlineItems = has(country) ? (OFFLINE[country] || []) : [];

  renderOnline(onlineItems);
  renderOffline(offlineItems);

  toggleGroup(shopGroup, shopContent, onlineItems.length);
  toggleGroup(storeGroup, storeContent, offlineItems.length);

  if (separator) separator.style.display = (onlineItems.length && offlineItems.length) ? '' : 'none';
  if (unavailable) unavailable.style.display = (!onlineItems.length && !offlineItems.length) ? '' : 'none';

  console.debug('[Fragrances WTB] cc=', country, 'online=', onlineItems.length, 'offline=', offlineItems.length);

  function renderOnline(list) {
    if (!shopContent) return;
    shopContent.innerHTML = '';
    list.forEach(it => {
      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className = 'c-group_item';
      item.innerHTML = `
        <div>${esc(it.name || '')}</div>
        <div class="c-group_item__link">
          <a data-wf--link--variant="black" href="${escAttr(it.url || '#')}" target="_blank" class="link c-underline w-inline-block">
            <div class="text-block">visit website</div>
          </a>
        </div>`;
      shopContent.appendChild(item);
    });
    if (shopGroup?.getAttribute('data-accordion') === 'open') {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => { shopContent.style.setProperty('--height', shopContent.scrollHeight + 'px'); });
    }
  }

  function renderOffline(list) {
    if (!storeContent) return;
    storeContent.innerHTML = '';
    list.forEach(it => {
      const item = document.createElement('div');
      item.className = 'c-group_item';
      const linkUrl = it.mapsUrl || it.url || '';
      const linkBlock = linkUrl ? `
        <div class="c-group_item__link">
          <a data-wf--link--variant="black" href="${escAttr(linkUrl)}" target="_blank" class="link c-underline w-inline-block">
            <div class="text-block">visit website</div>
          </a>
        </div>` : '';
      item.innerHTML = `
        <div>${esc(it.name || '')}</div>
        ${it.address ? `<div class="label color-dark">${esc(it.address)}</div>` : ''}
        ${linkBlock}`;
      storeContent.appendChild(item);
    });
    if (storeGroup?.getAttribute('data-accordion') === 'open') {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => { storeContent.style.setProperty('--height', storeContent.scrollHeight + 'px'); });
    }
  }

  function toggleGroup(group, content, count) {
    if (!group || !content) return;
    if (count) { group.style.display = ''; content.innerHTML = content.innerHTML || ''; }
    else { group.style.display = 'none'; content.innerHTML = ''; }
  }

  async function detectCountry() {
    try {
      const r = await fetch('https://api.ipinfo.io/lite/me?token=d064a1db5f2bae');
      const j = await r.json();
      console.log(j);
      const cc = (j && j.country_code) ? j.country_code.toUpperCase() : '';
      if (cc) return cc;
    } catch(e) {
    console.debug(e)
      const loc = (Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().locale || '').toUpperCase();
    const m = loc.match(/[-_](\w{2})/);
    const cc2 = m ? m[1] : '';
    return cc2 || '';
    }

  }

  function esc(s){ return String(s).replace(/[&<>]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;'}[c])); }
  function escAttr(s){ return String(s).replace(/"/g,'&quot;'); }
});

	opacity:0;
  pointer-events:none;
}

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const shopPanel = document.querySelector('.c-where_to_buy');
  const openShopPanelBtn = document.querySelector('#open-shop-panel');
  const closeShopPanelBtn = document.querySelector('#close-shop-panel');
  const shopPanelBackground = document.querySelector('.c-where_to_buy .c-where_to_buy__bg');
  const html = document.documentElement;

  const closeShopPanel = () => {
    shopPanel.classList.remove('is-active');
    lenis.start();
    html.classList.remove('s');
  };

  if (shopPanel && openShopPanelBtn && closeShopPanelBtn) {
    openShopPanelBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      shopPanel.classList.add('is-active');
      lenis.stop();
      html.classList.add('s');
    });

    closeShopPanelBtn.addEventListener('click', closeShopPanel);
    shopPanelBackground.addEventListener('click', closeShopPanel);
  }
});

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  document.querySelectorAll('.footer_title').forEach((title, i) => {
    const content = document.querySelectorAll('.footer_links')[i];

    title.addEventListener('click', function () {
      document.querySelectorAll('.footer_links').forEach((other, j) => {
        if (j !== i) {
          other.classList.remove('is-active');
          other.style.setProperty('--height', '0px');
        }
      });

      content.style.setProperty('--height', content.scrollHeight + 'px');
      content.classList.toggle('is-active');

      if (!content.classList.contains('is-active')) {
        content.style.setProperty('--height', '0px');
      }
    });
  });
});

</script></div></section><div class="custom w-embed w-script"><script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  function normalizePath(urlLike) {
    try {
      const u = new URL(urlLike, window.location.origin);
      let p = u.pathname;
      if (p.length > 1 && p.endsWith('/')) p = p.slice(0, -1);
      return p;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  const currentPath = normalizePath(window.location.href);
  if (!currentPath) return;

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-el="product-variant"] a[href]').forEach(function (a) {
    const linkPath = normalizePath(a.href);
    if (linkPath === currentPath) {
      const thumb = a.querySelector('.product_selection_thumnail');
      if (thumb) thumb.classList.add('active');
    }
  });
});
</script></div><script src="https://d3e54v103j8qbb.cloudfront.net/js/jquery-3.5.1.min.dc5e7f18c8.js?site=687ce6f184420067f31990d5" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha256-9/aliU8dGd2tb6OSsuzixeV4y/faTqgFtohetphbbj0=" crossorigin="anonymous"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/687ce6f184420067f31990d5/js/knr-davidoff.schunk.42849e839e7423c5.js" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha384-6Smz9Wjo+PH9mTCCd1k0/4NTbw13H8A8Rg/c9IzuhlCI4yXoS6f8/QTezORxyw0/" crossorigin="anonymous"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/687ce6f184420067f31990d5/js/knr-davidoff.639cf13d.ce0b18720b045249.js" type="text/javascript" integrity="sha384-BRJFirxkc+7TYwU29oe01n0eCzVvPGOTybqb+yq+j4NnQrx6rkZIN+N4mdSDSJzy" crossorigin="anonymous"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/gsap/3.15.0/gsap.min.js" type="text/javascript"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/gsap/3.15.0/ScrollTrigger.min.js" type="text/javascript"></script><script src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/gsap/3.15.0/SplitText.min.js" type="text/javascript"></script><script type="text/javascript">gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger,SplitText);</script><script src="https://unpkg.com/lenis@1.3.8/dist/lenis.min.js"></script>
<script>
    const lenis = new Lenis({
        lerp: 0.12,
    })

    // Synchronize Lenis scrolling with GSAP's ScrollTrigger plugin
    lenis.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update)

    // Add Lenis's requestAnimationFrame (raf) method to GSAP's ticker
    // This ensures Lenis's smooth scroll animation updates on each GSAP tick
    gsap.ticker.add((time) => {
        lenis.raf(time * 1000) // Convert time from seconds to milliseconds
    })

    // Disable lag smoothing in GSAP to prevent any delay in scroll animations
    gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)
</script>
<script>
	document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {

    const menu = document.querySelector('#menu')
    const openMenuBtn = document.querySelector('#open-menu')
    const closeMenuBtn = document.querySelector('#close-menu')
    const menuBackground = document.querySelector('#menu .c-menu_bg')
    const html = document.documentElement
    const panels = document.querySelector('.c-menu_panels');

    const closeMenu = () => {
        menu.classList.remove('is-active')
        lenis.start()
        html.classList.remove('s')
        const allPanels = document.querySelectorAll('[data-panel]')
        allPanels.forEach(panel => panel.classList.remove('is-active'));
        panels.classList.remove('is-active')
    }

    if (menu && openMenuBtn && closeMenuBtn) {
        openMenuBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            menu.classList.add('is-active')
            lenis.stop()
            html.classList.add('s')
        })

        closeMenuBtn.addEventListener('click', closeMenu)
        menuBackground.addEventListener('click', closeMenu)
    }
	})
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        const header = document.querySelector('.navbar-logo-center')
        const scrollY = window.scrollY
        const vh = window.innerHeight
        const vw = window.innerWidth

        if (scrollY > vh * 0.1) {
            header.classList.add('scrolled')
        } else {
            header.classList.remove('scrolled')
        }

        if (scrollY > vh && vw > 768) {
            header.classList.add('shrinked')
        } else {
            header.classList.remove('shrinked')
        }
    })
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const togglePanel = (slug, open = true) => {
        const panel = document.querySelector(`[data-panel="${slug}"]`);
        if (panel) panel.classList.toggle('is-active', open);
    };

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const openTrigger = e.target.closest('[data-panel-trigger]');
        const closeTrigger = e.target.closest('[data-close-panel]');

        if (openTrigger) {
            togglePanel(openTrigger.dataset.panelTrigger, true);
        }

        if (closeTrigger) {
            togglePanel(closeTrigger.dataset.closePanel, false);
        }
    });
});
</script>


<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const el = document.querySelector('[fs-list-element="results-count"], [fs-list-element="items-count"]');
  if (!el) return;

  let internal = false;

  const format = () => {
    const num = parseInt(el.textContent.trim(), 10);
    if (!Number.isFinite(num)) return;
    const desired = String(num).padStart(2, "0");
    if (el.textContent !== desired) {
      internal = true;
      el.textContent = desired;
      internal = false;
    }
  };

  format();

  const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
    if (!internal) format();
  });

  observer.observe(el, { childList: true, characterData: true });
});
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  if (!document.querySelector(".w-page-count")) {
    const el = document.querySelector(".pagination_page-wrapper");
    if (el) el.classList.add("hidden");
  }
});
</script>

<script>
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
	gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

document.querySelectorAll('.js-sentence').forEach(sentence => {
  const split = SplitText.create(sentence, {
    types: "lines",
    linesClass: "line-child",
    mask: "lines",
  });

  gsap.set(split.lines, { yPercent: 100 });

  const animOptions = {
    yPercent: 0,
    ease: "power4.out",
    stagger: { each: 0.1, from: "start" }
  };

  if (!sentence.hasAttribute("data-no-scroll")) {
    gsap.to(split.lines, {
      ...animOptions,
      scrollTrigger: {
        trigger: sentence,
        start: sentence.getAttribute("data-scrolltrigger-start") || "center center",
        end: sentence.getAttribute("data-scrolltrigger-end") || "bottom top",
        once: true
      }
    });
  } else {
    gsap.to(split.lines, {
      ...animOptions,
      delay: parseFloat(sentence.getAttribute("data-delay")) || .5
    });
  }
});


    // Refresh in case of virtual scroll / layout shifts
    setTimeout(() => {
      ScrollTrigger.refresh();
    }, 2000);
  });
</script>

<script>
function handleInput() {
  const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('.c-field__input, .c-field__textarea, .c-field__select');
  inputs.forEach(input => {
    input.addEventListener('focus', () => input.classList.add('has-value'));
    input.addEventListener('blur', () => {
      if (!input.value.trim()) input.classList.remove('has-value');
    });
  });
}
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', handleInput);
</script>





<script>
(function(){
  const mq=window.matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)');
  const container=document.querySelector('.c-product_thumbnail');
  const gallery=document.querySelector('.c-product_gallery');
  if(!container||!gallery)return;
  const thumbs=Array.from(gallery.querySelectorAll('[data-el="product-gallery-image"]'));
  if(!thumbs.length)return;
  let stack=container.querySelector('.c-product_stack');
  if(!stack){
    stack=document.createElement('div');
    stack.className='c-product_stack';
    container.appendChild(stack);
  }else{
    stack.innerHTML='';
  }
  const layers=thumbs.map((t,i)=>{
    const img=document.createElement('img');
    ['src','srcset','sizes','alt'].forEach(a=>{
      const v=t.getAttribute(a); if(v) img.setAttribute(a,v);
    });
    img.setAttribute('decoding','async');
    img.setAttribute('loading',i===0?'eager':'lazy');
    img.style.objectFit=i===0?'contain':'cover';
    stack.appendChild(img);
    return img;
  });
  const setActive=(idx)=>{
    layers.forEach((l,i)=>{
      l.classList.toggle('is-active',i===idx);
      l.style.objectFit=i===0?'contain':'cover';
    });
    thumbs.forEach((t,i)=>t.classList.toggle('is-active',i===idx));
  };
  const init=()=>{
    if(!mq.matches){
      layers.forEach(l=>l.classList.remove('is-active'));
      thumbs.forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('is-active'));
      return;
    }
    setActive(0);
  };
  gallery.addEventListener('click',(e)=>{
    const el=e.target.closest('[data-el="product-gallery-image"]');
    if(!el||!mq.matches)return;
    const idx=thumbs.indexOf(el);
    if(idx<0)return;
    if(layers[idx].classList.contains('is-active'))return;
    if(layers[idx].complete){
      setActive(idx);
    }else{
      layers[idx].addEventListener('load',()=>setActive(idx),{once:true});
    }
  },{passive:true});
  init();
  const onChange=()=>init();
  if(mq.addEventListener)mq.addEventListener('change',onChange);
  else mq.addListener(onChange);
})();
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
	const html = document.documentElement
  const filter = document.querySelector('#filter')
  const openFilterBtn = document.querySelector('#open-filter')
  const closeFilterBtn = document.querySelector('#close-filter')
  const applyFilterBtn = document.querySelector('#apply-filter')
  const filterBackground = document.querySelector('#filter .c-filter_bg')

  const closeFilter = () => {
    filter.classList.remove('is-active')
    lenis.start()
    html.classList.remove('s')
  }

  if (filter && openFilterBtn && closeFilterBtn && applyFilterBtn && filterBackground) {
    openFilterBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      filter.classList.add('is-active')
      lenis.stop()
      html.classList.add('s')
    })

    closeFilterBtn.addEventListener('click', closeFilter)
    applyFilterBtn.addEventListener('click', closeFilter)
    filterBackground.addEventListener('click', closeFilter)
  }
})
</script>
